Theorem 219.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: Let the cosmic structure S satisfy the conditions of convergence analysis framework. Under the given hypotheses, the mapping preserves the structural invariant with bound 642. Then the operator T satisfies ||T|| ≤ 577 and converges at rate O(1/n^1).

Proof: We apply the convergence analysis principle to the cosmic decomposition. By minimality, the element a^642 must satisfy the required bound, contradicting our assumption. As a consequence, the 577-dimensional case follows by the same argument. QED.
